### [1] [Fine-grained Computation-Communication Overlap via Tile-level Signaling and Scheduling for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19539)
*Minyu Cui,Anna Wingkvist,Morgan Ericsson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种细粒度的重叠执行方法，通过生产者-消费者协同设计将MoE模型中的专家计算与通信重叠，在4卡A100平台上实现了最高2.64倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE实现在分布式执行时，专家计算完成后才启动返回的all-to-all通信，导致通信延迟暴露在关键路径上，降低了GPU利用率。需要设计一种方法来重叠专家计算和通信，以提高分布式MoE模型的执行效率。

Method: 提出了一种基于tile级信号和调度的细粒度重叠执行方法，包含生产者-消费者协同设计：（1）持久化计算内核（生产者）覆盖rank上所有本地专家，消除重复内核启动开销并优先处理远程关键tile；（2）持久化通信内核（消费者）在专用的SM分区上，随着tile就绪以段粒度发起传输。该设计避免了对底层计算算子或通信原语的侵入式修改。

Result: 在4卡A100 GPU平台上，在三个MoE模型上对比四个最先进的MoE系统，该方法实现了最高2.64倍端到端加速和2.74倍MoE层加速。在各种GEMM形状、路由模式和广泛的生产者/消费者SM分区配置下，相比传统的非重叠基线，始终提高了算子级和MoE层级的性能。

Conclusion: 该细粒度重叠执行方法能够有效提高分布式MoE模型的执行效率，无需侵入式修改底层算子，在保持正确性的同时显著提升性能，对于大规模MoE模型的分布式部署具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures increase model capacity without proportionally increasing computation cost and have become a key building block for scaling large language models (LLMs) to trillion-parameter regimes. Efficient deployment of these MoE models relies on distributed execution across multiple GPUs, where each MoE layer involves two all-to-all communications: dispatching tokens to expert ranks and returning the expert outputs to their source ranks. Conventional MoE implementations launch this return all-to-all after expert compute completes, exposing communication latency on the critical path and reducing GPU utilization. We present a fine-grained approach that overlaps expert compute with the second all-to-all via tile-level signaling and scheduling. Our producer-consumer co-design combines: (1) a persistent per-rank computation kernel (producer) that covers all local experts on the rank to eliminate repeated kernel launch overhead and prioritizes remote-critical tiles, and (2) a persistent communication kernel (consumer) on a small dedicated partition of streaming multiprocessors (SMs) that issues segment-granular transfers as tiles become ready. Our co-design avoids intrusive changes to the underlying computation operators or communication primitives, making it practical for improving distributed MoE execution efficiency on multi-GPU systems. On a 4-A100 GPU platform, evaluated on three MoE models against four state-of-the-art MoE systems, our approach achieves up to 2.64x end-to-end speedup and 2.74x MoE-layer speedup. Compared with a conventional non-overlap baseline, our approach consistently improves both operator- and MoE-layer-level performance across varying GEMM shapes, router modes, and a broad range of producer/consumer SM partitions, while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [2] [Odin: Primitive-Level Synchronization for Distributed Point-Based Neural Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19893)
*Zhenxiang Ma,Zeyu He,Yuanzhen Zhou,Zhenyu Yang,Yuchang Zhang,Miao Tao,Rong Fu,Jidong Zhai,Hengjie Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Odin，一个分布式点基神经渲染(PBNR)训练系统，通过原语级同步替代全局屏障，在8 GPU上平均提升吞吐量1.22倍，隐藏82%关键路径等待时间，在64 GPU案例中相比Grendel提升吞吐量最高达1.89倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBNR系统采用原语索引依赖，每个视图仅读写视图相关的稀疏可变场景状态子集。大规模场景需要分布式训练，优化渲染器减少了每视图计算量，导致全局任务或迭代级屏障使同步而非渲染成为关键路径瓶颈。

Method: Odin采用原语级同步替代全局屏障：AOT调度器利用稳定局部性和阶段顺序识别低冲突重叠窗口；运行时在后续工作观察可变状态前验证原语发布；提供质量优先路径（保持同步训练可见性）和吞吐量优先路径（利用重叠和梯度证据仅允许小延迟、低影响读取）。

Result: 在4个现有PBNR管道和13个非城市场景的8 GPU实验中，Odin平均提升吞吐量1.22倍，隐藏82%关键路径等待时间并保持重建质量。在64 GPU的MatrixCity混合并行案例研究中，Odin相比Grendel提升吞吐量最高达1.89倍，且无需修改渲染器内核、优化器、训练预算或模型容量。

Conclusion: Odin通过原语级同步有效解决了PBNR分布式训练中的同步瓶颈问题，在保持重建质量的同时显著提升了系统吞吐量，为大规模场景重建和世界模型训练提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Point-based neural rendering (PBNR) represents 3D scenes as explicit, trainable primitives and underpins high-quality reconstruction and emerging embodied AI and world-model pipelines. Unlike layer-structured neural networks, PBNR has primitive-indexed dependencies: each view reads and updates only a sparse, view-dependent subset of mutable scene state. As large scenes require distributed training and optimized renderers reduce per-view computation, global task- or iteration-level barriers increasingly place synchronization, rather than rendering, on the critical path. We present Odin, a distributed PBNR training system that replaces global barriers with primitive-level synchronization. Its ahead-of-time scheduler uses stable locality and phase order to identify low-conflict overlap windows, while the runtime validates primitive publication before later work observes mutable state. Odin provides a quality-first path that preserves synchronized-training visibility and a throughput-first path that uses overlap and gradient evidence to admit only small, low-impact delayed reads; structural changes and high-impact cases remain synchronized. Across four existing PBNR pipelines and 13 non-city scenes on 8 GPUs, Odin improves throughput by 1.22 times on average and hides 82% of critical-path wait while preserving reconstruction quality. In a MatrixCity mixed-parallel case study scaling to 64 GPUs, Odin improves throughput over Grendel by up to 1.89 times without changing renderer kernels, optimizers, training budgets, or model capacity.

</details>


### [3] [Ascend to Science: Exploration of AI Chips for Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20120)
*Weicheng Xue,Kai Yang,Yongxiang Liu,Baisong Xu,Dengdong Fan,Xianglin Liu,Pengxiang Xu,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以昇腾910 NPU系列为代表，系统分析了AI张量加速器运行科学计算工作负载的瓶颈，提出并验证了针对HPL-MxP等五个应用场景的优化方法，证明通过协调处理数值表达、执行调度和数据传输，AI原生NPU可以支持科学计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI专用加速器的快速发展重塑了计算系统，围绕低精度张量引擎构建。然而HPC社区面临一个实际问题：这类硬件在何种条件下能够支持对数值稳健性、不规则内存访问和可扩展性有要求的科学计算工作负载？

Method: 以昇腾910 NPU系列为代表性张量中心平台，首先刻画阻碍科学代码直接部署的精度、执行和内存层次结构瓶颈，然后针对五个应用研究（HPL-MxP、LRSVD、SGEMM-cube、PQSim和SMC-X）开发并评估工作负载特定的映射方法，结合异构执行、混合精度数值公式、精度仿真、层次化内存协调以及通信-计算重叠等技术。

Result: 研究表明，当以协调的方式处理数值公式、执行部署和数据移动时，AI原生NPU可以实现数值稳健性、具有竞争力的性能和令人满意的扩展性。研究结果提供了科学工作负载如何适应张量中心架构的实践案例研究。

Conclusion: AI原生NPU可以成功支持科学计算工作负载，关键在于将数值公式、执行部署和数据移动作为整体进行优化。研究区分了可迁移的优化原则与昇腾特定的实现细节，为HPC社区利用AI加速器提供了实践指导。

Abstract: The rapid rise of AI-oriented accelerators has reshaped compute systems around low-precision tensor engines, raising a practical question for the HPC community: under what conditions can such hardware support scientific workloads that demand numerical robustness, irregular memory access, and scalability? Using the Ascend 910 NPU series as a representative tensor-centric platform, we characterize precision, execution, and memory-hierarchy bottlenecks that hinder the direct deployment of scientific codes. We then develop and evaluate workload-specific mappings across five application studies -- HPL-MxP, LRSVD, SGEMM-cube, PQSim, and SMC-X -- combining heterogeneous execution, mixed-precision numerical formulations, precision emulation, hierarchical memory orchestration, and communication--computation overlap. These studies show that AI-native NPUs can achieve numerical robustness, competitive performance, and satisfactory scalability when numerical formulation, execution placement, and data movement are addressed in a coordinated manner. Our results provide a state-of-the-practice case study of how scientific workloads can be adapted to tensor-centric architectures, while distinguishing transferable optimization principles from Ascend-specific implementation details.

</details>


### [4] [Fully Dynamic Rooted Spanning Tree on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20211)
*Abhijeet Sahu,Harmit Singh,Soham Nandy,G. Ramakrishna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了四种全新的全动态并行算法，用于在批量边插入/删除时高效更新生成森林，在GPU上实现了每秒200万次插入和140万次删除的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成树是图论中的基础结构，广泛应用于网络维护和路由调整等场景。现实网络的动态性要求在图演化时高效更新这些结构，特别是维护根生成树对于解决2-连通分量和最小权重生成树等算法至关重要。然而，该问题的并行算法研究几乎空白。

Method: 提出了四种全动态并行算法，用于在批量边插入或删除时更新生成森林，避免了从头重构的开销。实验在GPU环境下进行，使用了多种真实世界图数据集进行验证。

Result: 在GPU环境下的实验显示，算法达到了每秒200万次插入和140万次删除的吞吐量，显著优于现有的最先进并行静态算法。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了动态生成森林维护并行算法的空白，实验结果表明所提算法在真实图数据上具有优异的实际性能，为动态图算法领域提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Spanning trees are fundamental structures in graph theory, essential for various applications such as network maintenance, routing adjustments, and many more. The dynamic nature of real-world networks requires efficient updates to these structures as the underlying graph evolves. Maintaining rooted spanning trees dynamically is particularly crucial for algorithms addressing 2-connected components and minimum-weighted spanning trees. In this paper, we address the challenge of maintaining a rooted spanning forest when a batch of edges are inserted or deleted. We present four novel fully dynamic parallel algorithms to update the spanning forest without reconstructing it from scratch. To the best of our knowledge, parallel algorithms for this problem remain largely unexplored. Our experiments on a diverse collection of real-world graphs using a GPU environment demonstrate a throughput of 2 million insertions and 1.4 million deletions per second, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art parallel static algorithms.

</details>

### [5] [CHMAS: A Coupled Hierarchical Framework for Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19555)
*Dongming Wang,Jie Xu,Yanyu Zhang,Wei Ren*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了耦合分层多智能体系统(CHMAS)，通过双向信息流和异步更新协议解决多智能体强化学习中的全局协调与局部执行平衡问题，理论证明实现了O(log K/√K)的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习系统在平衡全局协调与局部执行方面面临根本性挑战，现有分层方法仅支持单向控制，缺乏战略规划与战术执行之间的有效反馈机制。

Method: 1) 设计分层架构：战略层每T步生成指导动作，战术层执行分布式策略；2) 引入耦合系数λ建立战术奖励对战略目标的反馈机制；3) 提出异步更新协议，战略参数每Nf个战术回合更新一次，使战术策略在战略变化间收敛到准平稳点；4) 提供双层规划框架及可加性近似方法。

Result: 理论证明在标准假设下异步方案在K次战略更新后达到O(log K/√K)收敛速率；多智能体觅食实验验证了空间分区探索策略的成功学习，双层稳定收敛。

Conclusion: CHMAS框架有效解决了分层多智能体学习中的非平稳性问题，双向信息流机制确保战略规划保持战术可行性，为复杂多智能体协调问题提供了理论与实际可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems face fundamental
  challenges in balancing global coordination with local execution
  across different temporal scales. This paper introduces the Coupled
  Hierarchical Multi-Agent System (CHMAS), a novel framework that
  decomposes multi-agent decision-making into centralized strategic
  planning and distributed tactical execution with bidirectional
  information flow. The strategic layer integrates all agents' states
  with an exclusive global environmental state to generate guidance
  actions every $T$ timesteps, while tactical agents execute
  distributed policies augmented by strategic guidance and local
  neighborhood observations. Unlike existing hierarchical approaches
  with unidirectional control, CHMAS establishes a feedback mechanism
  where accumulated tactical rewards influence strategic objectives
  through a coupling coefficient $λ$, ensuring strategic plans
  remain grounded in tactical feasibility. To address the
  non-stationarity inherent in hierarchical learning, we propose an
  asynchronous update protocol where strategic parameters update every
  $N_f$ tactical episodes, allowing tactical policies to converge to
  quasi-stationary points between strategic changes. We present both a
  general bi-level formulation capturing full system dynamics and a
  tractable additive approximation enabling rigorous analysis.
  Theoretical analysis proves that this asynchronous scheme achieves
  $\mathcal{O}(\log K/\sqrt{K})$ convergence for the strategic layer
  after $K$ strategic updates under standard assumptions. Experimental
  validation in a multi-agent foraging domain demonstrates successful
  learning of spatially partitioned exploration strategies, with both
  layers converging stably despite hierarchical coupling.

</details>


### [6] [Same Game, Different Story: A Minimal Conservative Strategic Robustness Benchmark for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19670)
*Seyed Pouyan Mousavi Davoudi,Alireza Amiri-Margavi,Amin Gholami Davodi,Hamidreza Hasani Balyani,Arshia Gharagozlou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了“同博弈，异叙事”基准，用于评估大型语言模型（LLM）在策略性环境中对博弈框架变化的鲁棒性。通过对GPT-3.5、GPT-4和LLaMa-2在四种社会困境博弈中的合作率进行二次分析，发现社交关系框架（朋友分享）相对于商业框架显著增加了合作率（提升0.307），综合策略鲁棒性为0.783。结果表明，即使行动集和收益固定，叙事框架也会显著影响LLM的行为，因此策略鲁棒性应与策略能力分开评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）在策略性环境中的应用日益增多，模型的行为可靠性成为关键问题。核心动机是探究模型在面对相同激励机制但不同叙事框架时，其决策是否保持一致，即模型的策略鲁棒性。这关系到LLM在实际多智能体交互中的可靠性和可预测性。

Method: 提出了“Same Game, Different Story”基准框架，将策略鲁棒性定义为在收益保持不变的框架变化下，模型诱导行动分布的不变性。通过对已发表的GPT-3.5、GPT-4和LLaMa-2在四种社会困境博弈中的合作率数据进行二次分析，比较商业框架和朋友分享框架下的行为差异。涵盖24个模型-博弈-语境单元和7200次决策，采用保守变换方法评估策略鲁棒性。

Result: 在保守变换下，综合策略鲁棒性为0.783，朋友分享框架相对于商业框架使合作率提升0.307。这表明即使底层行动集和收益保持不变，社交关系框架也能显著改变LLM的行为。模型对博弈的框架呈现敏感，策略鲁棒性不同于策略能力。

Conclusion: 策略鲁棒性应作为独立于策略能力的评估维度，建议使用一系列收益等价的提示族而非单一博弈呈现方式来评估。这有助于更全面地理解和提升LLM在策略性环境中的可靠性和一致性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate in strategic settings where outcomes depend on the actions of other agents. This raises a reliability question: will a model choose consistently when the same incentives are presented through different narratives? We introduce Same Game, Different Story, a benchmark that defines strategic robustness as invariance of model-induced action distributions under payoff-preserving changes in framing. We illustrate the framework through a secondary analysis of published aggregate cooperation rates for GPT-3.5, GPT-4, and LLaMa-2 across four social-dilemma games. The retained comparison covers business and friend-sharing framings, representing 24 model-game-context cells and 7,200 decisions in the source study. Because trial-level data were unavailable, approximate counts were reconstructed from published figures; the resulting estimates are therefore illustrative rather than an exact replication. Under the paper's conservative transformation, pooled strategic robustness is 0.783, and friend-sharing framing increases cooperation by 0.307 relative to business framing. The results indicate that social-relational framing can substantially alter LLM behavior even when the underlying action sets and payoffs remain fixed. Strategic robustness should therefore be evaluated separately from strategic competence, using families of payoff-equivalent prompts rather than a single presentation of a game.

</details>


### [7] [Not Birds of a Feather: Personality-Based Partner Selection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19785)
*Tao Wang,Hsiang-Ling Chiu,Chihang Wei,Yang Xiu,Zhonghao Hou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LLM智能体确实会基于人格特质选择合作伙伴，但这种选择呈现强烈的任务刻板印象，且与人类团队绩效证据不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多智能体LLM系统的发展，智能体越来越多地被赋予人格特质，并能自主选择合作伙伴。研究旨在探究在能力恒定的条件下，单独的大五人格特质是否会显著影响智能体的合作伙伴选择。

Method: 通过两项研究进行实验：研究1让中性主持智能体从六个候选原型（五种高特质人格+平衡控制组）中选择合作伙伴，涵盖五个任务类别共375次试验；研究2赋予主持智能体特定人格，测试其是否会表现出人类的相似性吸引效应。

Result: 发现LLM智能体的人格选择行为与人类存在显著差异：选择严重偏离随机分布且呈现任务刻板印象（开放型赢得100%创造性任务，尽责型在战略等任务中占优）；神经质型在分析任务中胜出；外向型、宜人型和平衡型几乎从不被选择；与人类相反，相似人格间的选择率低于随机水平(11.1% vs 16.7%)。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的人格选择机制真实存在且强烈，但表现出任务刻板化、非同质性，与人类团队绩效证据存在错误校准，这对智能体市场的偏见审计具有直接启示。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly let one agent choose which other agents to work with, and agents are increasingly given personalities through personas. We test whether Big Five personality alone influences partner selection when capability is explicitly held constant. Host agents chose among six validated candidate archetypes -- five marked high on one trait (openness, conscientiousness, extraversion, agreeableness, neuroticism) plus a balanced control -- presented with randomized names and ordering across five task categories (375 trials). With neutral hosts (Study 1, n=150), selection departed drastically from chance ($χ^2(5)=325.8$, $p<.001$), following a task-stereotype map: the open archetype won 100% of creative trials, the conscientious archetype 90-97% of strategic, synthesis, and problem-solving trials, and the neurotic archetype 37% of analytical trials (Cramer's V=.74); the extraverted, agreeable, and balanced archetypes were almost never chosen, although human meta-analyses identify team agreeableness as among the strongest personality predictors of team performance. With personality-assigned hosts (Study 2, n=225), and contrary to human similarity-attraction, self-similar partners were selected below chance (11.1% vs. 16.7%, p=.025) and at greater-than-chance trait distance (p<.0001); conscientious hosts diversified away from their own archetype, recruiting vigilant and open partners. Personality-based selection in LLM agents is real, strong, task-stereotyped, non-homophilous, and miscalibrated against human team-performance evidence -- with direct implications for bias auditing in agent marketplaces.

</details>


### [8] [Dreamer-CPC: Message Learning with World Models for Decentralized Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19809)
*Taisuke Takayama,Naoto Yoshida,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了Dreamer-CPC，一种基于世界模型的去中心化多智能体强化学习方法，通过在世界模型的潜在状态中学习消息，解决了现有方法仅依赖当前观测的问题，在观测缺失的任务中性能提升4-5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于表示学习的多智能体通信方法仅依赖当前观测生成消息，无法传递历史累积信息。在部分可观测环境下，智能体需要能够传达时间序列信息以支持更好的协作决策，但当前方法存在局限性。

Method: 提出Dreamer-CPC方法，将基于集体预测编码（CPC）的消息学习集成到DreamerV3的世界模型中。每个智能体独立维护世界模型和消息模块，从反映历史观测和动作的潜在状态中推断和交换消息，从而实现基于历史信息的通信。

Result: 在Observer非合作信息共享任务和新提出的CatchApple任务（任务相关观测暂时缺失）中，Dreamer-CPC显著优于基于当前观测生成消息的IPPO-CPC和无通信基线。特别是在CatchApple任务中，Dreamer-CPC的回报达到IPPO-CPC的4-5倍。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型潜在动态的通信机制能够在当前观测不足以支持决策时，有效支持去中心化决策，为部分可观测环境下的多智能体协作提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), inter-agent communication is effective for improving performance under partial observability. Representation learning-based approaches enable decentralized agents to learn messages grounded in their own observations, but they rely only on current observations and cannot convey information accumulated over time. We propose Dreamer-CPC, a decentralized model-based MARL method that integrates message learning based on Collective Predictive Coding (CPC) into the world model of DreamerV3. Each agent independently maintains a world model and a message module, and infers and exchanges messages from the latent states of the world model that reflect the history of past observations and actions. We evaluated Dreamer-CPC in two environments: Observer, a non-cooperative information-sharing task, and CatchApple, a newly introduced task in which task-relevant observations are temporarily missing. In both environments, Dreamer-CPC outperformed IPPO-CPC, an existing CPC-based method that generates messages from current observations, as well as no-communication baselines. In particular, in CatchApple, Dreamer-CPC achieved 4 to 5 times the episode return of IPPO-CPC, demonstrating effective coordination where other methods fail due to missing observations. These results suggest that communication grounded in the latent dynamics of world models can support decentralized decision-making when current observations alone are insufficient.

</details>


### [9] [Harnessing Disagreement: Detecting Correlated Agreement Blindness in Multi-Agent Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19899)
*Shay Seiya McDonnell,Avantika Singh,Quoc-Viet Pham,Vratislav Havlik,Gregory M. P. O'Hare*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多智能体仲裁系统中存在"相关性一致盲点"现象，即基础学习器性能提升导致模型趋同，反而削弱了对集中性相关失效的安全监控。提出的ARAT系统通过结合归纳随机森林、类比k近邻和校准元模型，将欠预测率从4.80%降至1.70%，有效缓解了这一风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体仲裁系统中，当基础学习器性能提升时往往会产生趋同现象，导致基于分歧触发升级的安全监控机制失效。作者识别出这一关键问题：当相关性失效集中发生时，分歧触发的升级机制可能产生结构性盲点，这种风险随着智能体流水线部署越来越强、相关性越高的模型而加剧。

Method: 提出ARAT（用于警报分类的仲裁推理智能体）系统，采用有向星形架构，结合三种组件：1）归纳随机森林（RF）智能体；2）类比案例k近邻（k-NN）智能体；3）校准元模型。系统通过保守性覆盖和安全标记门控机制来缓解相关性一致盲点效应。使用UNSW-NB15网络入侵检测数据集的82,332个留出样本进行验证，并在临床再入院数据集上进行跨数据集验证。

Result: 在UNSW-NB15数据集上，57.2%的错误发生在模型一致情况下，90.6%的危险欠预测在保守覆盖后仍能规避基于分歧的监控。消融实验显示增强基础学习器会增加错误相关性同时减少分歧。ARAT系统通过保守性覆盖（-2.6个百分点）和安全标记门控（-0.5个百分点），将欠预测率从软投票的4.80%降至1.70%。跨数据集验证支持这些指标，表明多样化仅在产生有效分歧而非趋同时才能改善安全性。

Conclusion: 分歧触发升级机制可能对相关性失效产生盲点，这种结构性风险随着智能体流水线部署越来越强、相关性越高的模型而加剧。研究结果表明，通过架构设计和保守性机制可以有效缓解这一问题，为多智能体系统的安全监控提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Disagreement-triggered escalation can create a structural blind spot in multi-agent arbitration: as base learners improve, they tend to converge, weakening safety monitoring where correlated failures concentrate. We term this correlated agreement blindness and present ARAT (Arbitrated Reasoning Agents for Alarm Triage), a directed-star system combining an inductive Random Forest (RF) agent, an analogical case-based k-nearest neighbour (k-NN) agent, and a calibrated meta-model to mitigate this effect. On 82,332 holdout samples from the UNSW-NB15 network intrusion detection dataset, 57.2% of errors occur under agreement and 90.6% of dangerous under-predictions evade disagreement-based monitoring even after conservative override; ablation shows that strengthening base learners increases error correlation while reducing disagreement. ARAT reduces under-prediction relative to soft voting from 4.80% to 1.70% via conservative override (-2.6pp) and a safety-flag gate (-0.5pp), demonstrating architectural gains. Cross-dataset validation on clinical readmission supports these indicators, suggesting that diversification improves safety only when it generates productive disagreement rather than convergence. These results indicate that disagreement-triggered escalation can be blind to correlated failure, a risk that may intensify as agentic pipelines deploy increasingly capable, correlated models.

</details>

### [10] [Native Multi-Dimensional Subquadratic Operators via Input Dependent Long Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19378)
*David R. Wessels,Farhad Ramezanghorbani,David W. Romero,Alireza Moradzadeh,Olivia Viessmann,Maksim Zhdanov,John St. John,Ken Janik,David M Knigge,Yucheng Tang,Erik J Bekkers,Saee Gopal Paliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了HyenaND，一种次二次复杂度的全局输入依赖算子，通过隐式参数化的多维卷积核直接处理多维数据，在多个领域达到或超越注意力机制的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的次二次复杂度注意力替代方案在处理多维数据时存在缺陷：标准卷积缺乏全局感受野和输入依赖性，而循环模型需要将图像、体积和PDE等数据栅格化为1D扫描顺序，这破坏了数据的空间结构。

Method: 引入HyenaND算子，通过隐式参数化的全局、输入依赖的多维卷积核直接作用于多维数据的原生几何结构。提供了CUDA实现nSubQ，融合FFT卷积路径将O(L log L)复杂度转化为实际的时钟加速。

Result: 在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像和PDE建模等任务中，纯HyenaND堆栈匹配强注意力基线的准确性，而HyenaND与注意力层交替的混合配置优于纯注意力和强循环混合模型。

Conclusion: HyenaND成功解决了多维数据处理中次二次复杂度算子的局限性，在保持原生数据几何结构的同时实现了全局建模能力，为长序列建模提供了高效的注意力替代方案。

Abstract: Subquadratic alternatives to attention require compromises when applied to multi-dimensional data: standard convolutions lack global receptive fields and input dependency, while recurrent models require rasterizing data such as images, volumes, and partial differential equation (PDE) into an ad-hoc $1\rm D$ scan order that violates their spatial structure. We introduce \textit{HyenaND}, a subquadratic, global, input-dependent operator that acts directly on the native geometry of multidimensional data through convolutions with implicitly parametrized global, input-dependent multi-dimensional convolutional kernels. Our CUDA implementation, \texttt{nSubQ}, fuses the FFT-convolution path to turn HyenaND's $\mathcal{O}(L \log L)$ scaling into wall-clock speedups. Across long-context genomics, computer vision, medical imaging, and PDE modeling, pure HyenaND stacks match the accuracy of strong attention baselines, while hybrid configurations that interleave HyenaND and attention layers outperform both pure attention and strong recurrence-based hybrids.

</details>


### [11] [Bayesian Wind Tunnels for Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19379)
*Siddhartha R Dalal,Vishal Misra,Abhay Parekh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer是否能执行贝叶斯模型选择，发现小规模模型在关系任务上可达到贝叶斯最优，但在需要算术的感知任务上存在语义稳定性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明Transformer能在固定假设类内执行精确贝叶斯过滤，但能否从数据中识别正确的假设类（贝叶斯模型选择）尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 引入模型选择贝叶斯风洞环境，使用不动点自由对合（f(f(x))=x）等关系性质任务，对比整数token与不透明符号两种输入，并设计平稳性控制和表头子任务诊断。

Result: 2.8M参数模型在关系任务上达到0.01比特熵的贝叶斯最优；在需要算术的感知任务上，整数token成功但不透明符号完全失败，112倍规模扩展后边界仍存在；平稳性控制证实稳定语义是关键因素。

Conclusion: Transformer能执行真正的模型选择（超越简单性/子集偏置），但存在明确的感知访问条件边界：算术操作需要稳定语义支持，该边界在规模扩展下持续存在；前沿LLM显示定性贝叶斯行为但存在约55倍校准差距。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that transformers can perform exact Bayesian filtering within a fixed
  hypothesis class. Can they also perform Bayesian model selection -- identifying the correct
  hypothesis class from data? We introduce model-selection Bayesian wind tunnels: controlled
  environments where ground-truth posteriors over hypothesis classes are available in closed
  form. Using fixed-point-free involutions -- whose defining property f(f(x))=x is purely
  relational -- a 2.8M-parameter transformer achieves 0.01-bit entropy agreement with the
  Bayesian optimum (3 seeds), with both integer tokens and opaque symbols whose meanings
  change every episode. This extends to non-nested comparisons: involutions vs. 3-cycles
  (where neither class is a subset of the other) achieve class-posterior MAE under 0.001,
  demonstrating genuine model selection beyond simplicity/subset bias. We then identify a
  sharp perceptual access condition: when the discriminative statistic requires arithmetic --
  modular addition (rotations) or multiplication (f(x)=cx mod p) -- model selection succeeds
  with integer tokens but fails completely with opaque symbols, and this boundary persists
  under 112x scaling (2.8M to 316M parameters). A stationarity control confirms the operative
  factor: opaque tokens with a fixed relabeling succeed (0.009-bit MAE), showing that stable
  semantics, not integer identity, enable circuit compilation. Header subtask diagnostics
  localize the failure to the composition of header inversion with arithmetic rather than
  header parsing itself. Probing frontier LLMs on the same tasks shows qualitative Bayesian
  behavior but a large calibration gap (~55x), measured through lossy probes and therefore
  directional rather than exact.

</details>


### [12] [CruiseBench: A Real-Flight-Aligned N-CMAPSS Benchmark for Engine RUL Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19380)
*Pu Cheng,Qiang Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CruiseBench基准，通过引入CPM-N-CMAPSS掩码从N-CMAPSS数据集提取巡航阶段数据，解决了全飞行记录数据量过大且退化信号与运行状态耦合的问题，为RUL预测提供了可控的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-CMAPSS数据集通过保留完整的飞行时间序列增加了真实感，但也导致评估控制性降低：数据量增加、退化信号与运行状态变化纠缠，使得预处理选择和模型性能对比变得困难。

Method: 提出CruiseBench巡航阶段RUL基准，引入CPM-N-CMAPSS掩码存储通过公共高度法识别的巡航区间。采用固定协议：使用场景描述符和测量传感器作为输入，排除虚拟传感器和健康参数，保留原生分辨率窗口，应用数据集级RUL上限。

Result: 在CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10设置下，TSMixer取得最低平均RMSE（3.4±1.71）和Saxena评分（(2.50±2.99)×10⁴）。消融实验表明飞行阶段选择、时间降采样方法和RUL上限阈值会影响结果。

Conclusion: CruiseBench提供了可复现的子基准用于可控的RUL模型对比，CPM-N-CMAPSS为未来的迁移学习和域适应研究提供了阶段特定的数据基础。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction estimates how long an engine can continue safe operation and is central to maintenance planning. N-CMAPSS extends C-MAPSS by simulating run-to-failure aero-engine trajectories using recorded real-flight profiles and retaining complete within-flight time series rather than cycle-level snapshots. However, this added realism reduces evaluation control because full-flight records increase data volume and entangle degradation cues with operating-regime variation, complicating preprocessing choices and direct comparisons of RUL modeling performance. To mitigate this issue, this paper proposes CruiseBench, a cruise-stage RUL benchmark derived from N-CMAPSS. It introduces CPM-N-CMAPSS (Cruising-Period Mask for N-CMAPSS), a mask artifact that stores cycle-local cruising intervals identified by the common-altitude method for the nine accessible subdatasets. CruiseBench applies a fixed protocol to the masked rows, using scenario descriptors and measured sensors as inputs while excluding virtual sensors, health parameters, and auxiliary metadata from the feature tensor, preserving native-resolution windows, and applying dataset-wise RUL caps. Experiments with LSTM, GRU, TCN, and TSMixer provide baseline results for this setting. Under CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10, TSMixer obtains the lowest average RMSE, $3.4\pm1.71$, and Saxena score, $(2.50\pm2.99)\times 10^{4}$. Ablation studies show that flight-stage selection, temporal downscaling method, and RUL-cap threshold affect reported results. With its fixed cruise-stage protocol, CruiseBench provides a reproducible sub-benchmark for controlled RUL model comparison and CPM-N-CMAPSS provides a stage-specific data foundation for future transfer-learning and domain-adaptation studies.

</details>


### [13] [Autonomous Collaborative Learning Among an Ensemble of Tsetlin Machines with Consensus-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20124)
*Yehuda Rudin,Osnat Keren,Michal Yemini,Alexander Fish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于共识推理的去中心化协作学习范式，使多个Tsetlin Machine能够在垂直特征分区设置下进行分布式学习，同时保持数据隐私。实验表明该方法在分类准确率上与集中式模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管近期有研究探讨Tsetlin Machine联邦学习，但分布式和去中心化TM学习这一更广泛领域在现有文献中尚未受到足够关注，值得进一步探索。需要一种能够在保护数据隐私的前提下实现多代理协作学习的方案。

Method: 提出一种在垂直特征分区设置下的去中心化协作学习范式，采用基于共识的推理机制。每个代理维护私有TM模型，代理间不交换原始数据。推理阶段通过共识机制将各代理的模型预测组合成全局共识，支持具有不同数据获取方式、本地数据分布或计算资源的异构TM代理。

Result: 在二维网格和连通图网络拓扑上进行的实验表明，该方法实现的分类准确率与集中式模型相当。

Conclusion: 该去中心化范式成功实现了Tsetlin Machine的协作学习，在保护数据隐私的同时保持了与集中式模型相当的性能，为多模态传感环境等场景中的信息集成与融合提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a rule-based machine-learning algorithm comprising collectives of two-action Tsetlin Automata (TAs) that cooperatively form conjunctive logical clauses from Boolean inputs through stochastic feedback. Although few recent studies have examined TM Federated Learning, the broader area of distributed and decentralized TM learning has not received much attention in the existing literature and warrants further exploration. In this work, we propose a paradigm for decentralized collaborative learning under a vertical feature-partitioning setting among an ensemble of Tsetlin Machines using consensus-based inference. Within this decentralized paradigm, each agent maintains its own private TM model, and there is no exchange of raw data among agents. Inference combines individual agents model predictions into a global consensus. The paradigm accommodates heterogeneous TM-based agents with differing data acquisition means, local data distributions, or computational resources, thereby facilitating the integration and fusion of information in settings such as multi-modal sensing environments. Experiments conducted using two-dimensional grid and connected graph network topologies demonstrate that the classification accuracies achieved are comparable to those of centralized models.

</details>


### [14] [Air Quality Arena: A Large-Scale Multi-Region Ground Monitoring Dataset and Benchmark for Air Quality Forecasting with Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19381)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Manik Gupta,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Air Quality Arena (AQA)，这是一个大规模、多国家、多污染物的空气质量数据集和基准测试平台，用于评估时间序列基础模型(TSFMs)在空气质量预测中的表现。实验表明TSFMs作为零样本预测器表现优异，超越了传统基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染每年造成约790万过早死亡，准确预测空气质量是关键的公共卫生优先事项。现有基准在地理范围和污染物覆盖方面过于狭窄，且未能在真实世界大规模数据上评估最新一代时间序列基础模型的性能。

Method: 构建了AQA-Data数据集，覆盖7个国家、4大洲、跨越3年时间的6种主要污染物，包含超过14,000个站点-污染物时间序列。创建了AQA-Bench基准测试，对11个领先的时间序列基础模型和经典基线模型进行了系统评估，专注于短期空气质量预测任务。

Result: 时间序列基础模型作为零样本预测器表现有效，持续优于经典基线模型。表现最佳的模型采用跨模态架构，利用视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测。AQA已在AirQualityArena.github.io公开发布。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了空气质量预测领域大规模、多地域基准测试的空白，证明了时间序列基础模型在空气质量预测中的有效性，为未来研究和实际应用提供了重要的公开资源和评估框架。

Abstract: Air pollution causes an estimated 7.9 million premature deaths annually, making accurate forecasting a critical public health priority. Machine learning is increasingly being applied to forecast air pollution levels, yet existing benchmarks remain narrow in both geographic scope and pollutant coverage, and fail to evaluate the latest generation of time series foundation models (TSFMs) on real world, large scale data. We present Air Quality Arena (AQA), a large scale multi-country and multi-pollutant dataset (AQA-Data) and benchmark (AQA-Bench) to address this gap. AQA covers 6 major pollutants over a three year period across 7 diverse countries and 4 continents, with more than 14,000 station-pollutant series, aiming to provide a comprehensive benchmark for air quality tasks. We benchmark this dataset across 11 leading time series foundation models and classical baselines to assess performance on short-term air quality forecasting. Our results demonstrate that TSFMs are effective zero-shot forecasters and consistently outperform classical baselines, with our top-performing model employing a cross-modal architecture that leverages a vision foundation model for time series forecasting. AQA is publicly released at AirQualityArena.github.io

</details>


### [15] [Challenges of Explainability in Continual Learning for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19382)
*Quentin Besnard,Emmanuel Doumard,Nicolas Labroche,Nicolas Ragot,Nicolas Ringuet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在持续学习框架下，利用可解释性方法（注意力 rollout 和 Grad-CAM）来理解和改进时间序列预测模型的适应性，通过实验揭示了归因模式随时间的演化规律及其对数据选择策略的指导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列预测中展现出强大潜力，但由于非平稳动态特性和可解释性有限，在实际环境监测中的部署仍面临挑战。作者旨在将可解释性作为核心工具，来理解自适应时间序列预测中的持续学习行为。

Method: 研究了 PatchMixer、PatchTST 和 DLinear 等神经预测架构，引入基于注意力的采样机制支持模型随时间适应。采用 Experience Replay 策略，通过注意力 rollout 和基于梯度的归因方法（Grad-CAM）分析持续学习框架内的预测行为和采样策略。实验在表现出异质模式和制度转换的真实世界测压时间序列上进行。

Result: 实验表明，分析模型和采样行为能为持续学习框架的动态特性提供有价值的见解。结果揭示了归因模式随时间的演化方式，以及这些模式如何指导非平稳预测场景中的数据选择和适应策略。

Conclusion: 除了预测性能，本研究强调了利用可解释性理解持续学习行为的挑战与机遇，为非平稳环境下的时间序列预测模型适应提供了新的见解和方法论支持。

Abstract: Deep learning models have shown strong potential for time series forecasting, yet their deployment in real-world environmental monitoring remains challenging due to non-stationary dynamics and limited explainability. In this work, we investigate explainability as a central tool for understanding continual learning in adaptive time series forecasting, with Experience Replay strategies. We study neural forecasting architectures such as PatchMixer, PatchTST and DLinear, augmented with attention-based sampling mechanisms to support model adaptation over time. Explainability is leveraged through attention rollout and gradient-based attribution methods (Grad-CAM) to analyze both predictive behavior and sampling strategies within a continual learning framework. Experiments conducted on real-world piezometric time series exhibiting heterogeneous patterns and regime shifts show that analyzing model and sampling behaviors provides valuable insights into the dynamics of the continual learning framework. Beyond predictive performance, our results highlight the challenges and opportunities of using explainability to understand continual learning behaviors, revealing how attribution patterns evolve over time and how they can inform data selection and adaptation strategies in non-stationary forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [16] [AlphaRoute: Large Language Models as Semantic Optimizers for Multi-Objective Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19768)
*Kabir Murjani,Mishri Bhavsar,Manish I. Patel,Jonti Talukdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlphaRoute，一个基于LLM的多目标自适应全局路由框架，在ISPD 2025基准测试上实现了显著的溢出减少，优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统启发式方法依赖静态惩罚策略，在复杂拥塞拓扑上表现不佳，无法有效解决VLSI全局路由这一NP-hard组合优化问题。

Method: 提出AlphaRoute框架，将重布线（R&R）重构为动态优化系统。引入SHAP溢出分解方法隔离每网拥塞，通过3D Dijkstra迷宫布线和自适应PathFinder策略驱动目标子图提取。关键创新是使用大语言模型（LLM）作为语义策略优化器，在确定性知识图谱约束下解释拥塞指标并动态调整惩罚参数。

Result: 在ISPD 2025基准测试上：MEMPOOL基准上溢出减少98.6%；在受限的ARIANE设计上实现溢出146,109（相比现有技术减少29.8倍），惩罚分数S_orig = 0.0538，显著优于SOTA的1.780。

Conclusion: 优越的算法搜索几何设计可以克服解释型Python实现的延迟问题，证明了LLM驱动的动态策略优化在VLSI全局路由中的有效性。

Abstract: Very Large Scale Integration (VLSI) global routing is an NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem requiring signal net assignment across capacity-constrained 3D grids while minimizing congestion, wirelength, and via transitions. Because traditional heuristics rely on static penalty schedules that fail on complex congestion topologies, we present AlphaRoute: a multi-objective adaptive search framework reformulating rip-up and reroute (R&R) into a dynamic optimization system. We introduce SHAP-based overflow decomposition to isolate per-net congestion, driving targeted subgraph extraction via 3D Dijkstra maze routing and an adaptive PathFinder policy. Crucially, AlphaRoute employs Large Language Models (LLMs) as semantic policy optimizers. Bounded by a deterministic knowledge graph, the LLMs interpret congestion metrics to dynamically adjust penalty parameters. Evaluated on ISPD 2025 benchmarks, AlphaRoute reduces overflow by 98.6% on MEMPOOL. On the constrained ARIANE design, we achieve an overflow of 146,109 (a 29.8x reduction in overflow over the state of the art), yielding a penalized score of S_orig = 0.0538 versus the State-of-the-art (SOTA) 1.780. These results demonstrate that superior algorithmic search geometry can overcome the latency of interpreted Python implementations.

</details>

*Akarsh K Nair,Muhammad Arifur Rahman,Nicholas Shopland,Andy Burton,Jun He,Yuan Shen,David Baldwin,Emma O'Dowd,Amna Burzic,Mufti Mahmud,David J. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SynPre-FL框架，通过结合高保真合成EHR数据生成与联邦学习预训练，解决了临床风险预测中的数据稀缺、客户端异质性和类别不平衡问题，在非IID条件下显著提升了模型的鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在隐私保护的医疗风险预测中具有潜力，但其部署受限于数据共享限制、客户端异质性、类别不平衡以及缺乏现实的表格型电子健康记录(EHR)基准。虽然合成数据生成可以缓解数据稀缺问题，但其与联邦优化的整合尚未得到系统性研究。

Method: 提出SynPre-FL统一框架：1) 使用潜在自编码器-扩散模型生成隐私保护的合成队列；2) 用合成数据热启动联邦训练；3) 采用异质性感知优化，包括类别平衡的局部目标、近端正则化和自适应服务器聚合；4) 引入事后校准和联邦安全可解释性支持。

Result: 合成生成器在保护单变量、双变量和多变量结构的同时，能够抵御成员推理和重构攻击。在TSTR、TRTS和基于模型的评估中，生成的数据具有良好的下游效用。在5、10和15个异构客户端的联邦设置中，SynPre-FL在鲁棒性和可扩展性方面均优于基线方法，特别是在严重的非IID分片条件下。校准提高了概率可靠性，SHAP分析产生了稳定且临床一致的特征归因。

Conclusion: SynPre-FL为结合合成数据与联邦学习提供了一个实用且可复现的框架，能够实现隐私感知、可解释且鲁棒的临床预测，适用于分布式表格型EHR数据，为医疗领域的联邦学习实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) offers a promising approach to privacy-preserving clinical risk prediction, but its deployment remains limited by restricted data sharing, client heterogeneity, class imbalance, and the lack of realistic tabular electronic health record (EHR) benchmarks. Synthetic data generation may alleviate data scarcity, yet its integration with federated optimisation has received limited systematic study. We propose SynPre-FL, a unified framework combining high-fidelity synthetic EHR generation with synthetic-pretrained FL for robust prediction under non-IID conditions. A latent autoencoder-diffusion model generates privacy-preserving synthetic cohorts, which are used to warm-start federated training. This pretraining is followed by heterogeneity-aware optimisation using class-balanced local objectives, proximal regularisation, and adaptive server aggregation. Post-hoc calibration and federated-safe explainability support reliable and interpretable risk estimates. Experiments show that the synthetic generator preserves univariate, bivariate, and multivariate structure while protecting against membership-inference and reconstruction attacks. The generated data achieve strong downstream utility under TSTR, TRTS, and model-based evaluations. Across federated settings with 5, 10, and 15 heterogeneous clients, SynPre-FL consistently improves robustness and scalability over baseline methods, especially under severe non-IID fragmentation. Calibration improves probability reliability, while SHAP analysis produces stable and clinically coherent feature attributions across federation sizes. SynPre-FL therefore provides a practical and reproducible framework for combining synthetic data with FL to enable privacy-aware, interpretable, and robust clinical prediction from distributed tabular EHR data.

</details>


### [18] [SUM: Unified Geometric Surgery on Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors for Federated Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19384)
*Jaeik Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SUM框架，通过在服务器端对适应向量进行几何手术，有效解决联邦类增量学习中的时空灾难性遗忘问题，在多个基准测试上实现高达22%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦类增量学习（FCIL）面临空间干扰（来自异构客户端）和时序干扰（来自顺序任务）的双重挑战，导致时空灾难性遗忘（ST-CF）。现有方法通常采用分离机制处理时空干扰，增加了客户端计算或通信开销，且未能调节聚合时更新方向间的相互作用。

Method: 将FCIL重新解释为统一的多任务学习问题，提出统一时空适应向量几何手术框架（SUM）。该纯服务器端框架在聚合时对适应向量执行几何手术：空间SUM缓解每轮内的客户端级干扰，因果在线时序SUM消除跨任务干扰，无需额外的客户端计算、通信或内存开销。

Result: SUM在多种视觉和语言基准测试中相比现有FCIL方法实现高达22%的性能提升，同时保持对不可靠客户端的鲁棒性和计算效率。

Conclusion: SUM为FCIL提供了一种高效的服务器端解决方案，通过几何手术有效调节时空干扰，在不增加客户端负担的前提下显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: Real-world intelligent systems often require both distributed collaboration across data-isolated clients and continual adaptation to evolving tasks. This setting naturally gives rise to Federated Class Incremental Learning (FCIL), which combines Federated Learning (FL) and Continual Learning (CL). However, their combination introduces two coupled sources of interference: spatial interference from heterogeneous clients and temporal interference from sequential tasks, jointly leading to Spatial-Temporal Catastrophic Forgetting (ST-CF). Existing approaches typically address spatial and temporal interference with separate mechanisms, often incurring additional client-side computation or communication, while leaving directional interactions among updates during aggregation unregulated. In this paper, we reinterpret FCIL as a unified multi-task learning problem, where both client and task updates are represented as adaptation vectors in a shared parameter space. Based on this view, we propose Unified Geometric Surgery on Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors (SUM), a purely server-side framework that performs geometric surgery on adaptation vectors during aggregation. Spatial SUM mitigates client-level interference within each round, while causal online temporal SUM removes cross-task interference over time without additional client-side computation, communication, or memory beyond standard federated training. Empirically, SUM achieves up to 22% improvement over prior FCIL methods across diverse vision and language benchmarks while remaining robust to unreliable clients and maintaining computational efficiency.

</details>


### [19] [STN-TGAT: Top-K Portfolio Construction via Prior-Guided Graph Attention with Learnable Soft-Threshold Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19385)
*Haoran Guo,Yutong Lu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STN-TGAT模型用于股票排序和投资组合构建，结合时间Transformer与图注意力网络，在考虑交易成本等现实约束下实现了优于基准模型的预测准确性和投资回报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现实投资环境下股票排序与投资组合构建问题，现有方法难以同时建模时间动态与截面依赖关系，且缺乏对实际交易约束的考量。

Method: 提出软阈值NMI先验Transformer图注意力网络（STN-TGAT），将时间Transformer与图注意力网络集成以捕获长时序模式和股票间动态关系；引入基于NMI的先验图和软阈值稀疏化机制以降低噪声相关性并保留信息连接；在标普500前50只股票中选择Top-5进行组合，进行权重分配与交易成本调整。

Result: 在真实数据上的实证研究表明，STN-TGAT在预测准确性和投资组合回报率方面均持续优于基准模型。

Conclusion: 将决策对齐训练与自适应关系建模相结合，为数据驱动的投资组合构建提供了一种连贯且实用的有效框架。

Abstract: This paper tackles the problem of stock ranking and portfolio construction under realistic investment settings by jointly modeling temporal dynamics and cross-sectional dependencies. We propose the Soft-Threshold NMI-prior Transformer Graph Attention Network (STN-TGAT), which integrates a temporal Transformer with a Graph Attention Network to capture long-horizon sequential patterns and dynamic inter-stock relationships. An NMI-based prior graph combined with a soft-threshold sparsification mechanism enhances structural robustness by mitigating noisy correlations while preserving informative connections. The portfolio formation process incorporates practical considerations, including Top-5 selection within the Top-50 $S\&P$ 500 constituents, explicit weight allocation, and transaction cost adjustment, thereby aligning the evaluation with real-world trading conditions. Empirical results on real-world data demonstrate that STN-TGAT consistently outperforms benchmark models from predictive accuracy and investment profitability measured by portfolio returns. These findings suggest that combining decision-aligned training with adaptive relational modeling provides a coherent and practically effective framework for data-driven portfolio construction.

</details>


### [20] [Building Fast, Evaluating Slow: Pipeline Choices Dominate Autointerpretability Score Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19386)
*Sinie van der Ben,Neele Roch,Anna Hedström,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验发现稀疏自编码器（SAE）的可解释性评估中，方法变异性超过架构变异性，评估指标存在不稳定性，跨论文比较可能反映的是评估流程差异而非架构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前跨论文比较稀疏自编码器（SAE）可解释性主要依赖自动可解释性评分。为使这些比较有意义，评分必须反映特征的稳定属性而非评估流程中的混杂因素。作者旨在验证这一假设是否成立。

Method: 在四种评估指标（仿真、检测、模糊测试、纯度）、两个模型（Pythia-160M、Apertus-8B）和四个方法变化轴线上进行系统性实验，分析自动可解释性评分的稳定性。

Result: 发现方法变异性在所有指标和测试模型中集体超过架构变异性；各指标表现出不同的不稳定性特征，其中检测指标最稳定，模糊测试在所有条件下不可靠；top-k特征排名在语料库和绘制条件间不一致，稳定的平均分数掩盖了每个特征的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于自动可解释性评分的跨论文比较可能反映的是评估流程差异而非架构差异。这影响了关于SAE实用性的持续辩论。不可靠的评估减缓了可解释性研究的进展。作者贡献了方差分解方法、稳定性检查和最小报告清单以支持评估。

Abstract: Cross-paper comparison of sparse autoencoder (SAE) interpretability often relies on autointerpretability scores. In this evaluation pipeline, a language model (LM) explains each feature, and another LM scores the explanation. For these comparisons to be meaningful, scores must reflect stable properties of the features rather than confounding aspects of the evaluation pipeline. Through systematic experiments across four metrics (simulation, detection, fuzzing, purity), two models (Pythia-160M, Apertus-8B), and four axes of methodological variation, we show that this assumption does not hold. Specifically, we find that R1) methodological variance collectively exceeds architectural variance across all metrics and tested models; R2) each metric exhibits a distinct instability profile, with detection being the most stable and fuzzing unreliable across all conditions; R3) top-k feature rankings do not stay consistent across corpus and draw conditions, masking per-feature instability behind stable mean scores; a failure that cannot be detected by monitoring explanation similarity alone. These findings suggest that cross-paper comparisons based on autointerpretability scores may reflect pipeline differences rather than architectural differences, with implications for the ongoing debate on SAE utility. More broadly, unreliable evaluation slows progress in interpretability research at a time when reliable tools for understanding AI systems are needed. To support evaluation, we contribute a variance decomposition approach, a Stability Check, and a Minimum Reporting Checklist.

</details>


### [21] [Scale-Aware Learning of Chaotic Dynamics on Unstructured Meshes via Binned Spectral Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19387)
*Kanad Sen,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GLEAM方法，将频谱功率损失扩展到非结构化网格，通过图拉普拉斯频率带和切比雪夫多项式滤波器实现可扩展的尺度感知监督，显著提升了混沌动力系统的长期预测精度和统计不变性保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非线性动力系统的代理模型需要同时保持点精度和物理场的尺度依赖结构。现有的频谱功率损失（如分箱频谱损失）仅适用于结构化网格的傅里叶分解，但实际工程中广泛使用非结构化网格，缺乏标准的傅里叶基，亟需一种适用于非结构化网格的频谱监督机制。

Method: 提出三种核心技术：（1）用图拉普拉斯频率带替代傅里叶带，通过图拉普拉斯特征空间实现频谱功率匹配；（2）利用稀疏切比雪夫多项式图滤波器避免昂贵的特征分解，实现可扩展的频带投影近似；（3）提出GLEAM方法，在多层图架构上应用保留子空间尺度感知监督，实现跨层级的统计不变性保持。

Result: 在湍流预测任务上验证表明：所提频谱损失显著改善了非结构化网格上的长期推演保真度；相比确定性基线方法，能够更好地保持统计不变量；切比雪夫近似和多层架构在保持性能的同时提供了良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 成功将频谱功率损失扩展至非结构化网格，为混沌动力系统的代理建模提供了有效的尺度感知监督机制。所提方法在长期预测精度和统计特性保持方面展现出优越性能，为复杂几何区域上的动力系统预测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Surrogate modeling for high-dimensional nonlinear dynamical systems that exhibit chaos requires mechanisms that preserve not only pointwise accuracy but also the scale-dependent structure of physical fields. Bandwise spectral power losses, such as the binned spectral loss function, provide such supervision on structured grids, where Fourier modes define a standard frequency decomposition. On irregular meshes, however, no canonical Fourier basis exists, and spectral representations must be constructed from graph operators induced by mesh connectivity and geometry. In this study, we extend the binned spectral power loss for application to unstructured-mesh surrogate modeling of nonlinear dynamical systems. This is obtained by replacing Fourier bands with graph-Laplacian frequency bands, and we provide scalable Chebyshev and multilevel approximations for improving long-horizon rollout fidelity. In its full-spectrum form, our approach uses graph Laplacian eigenspaces to provide a graph analogue of Fourier band-power matching, but incurs the high cost of spectral decomposition. As a scalable approximation, we replace exact band projectors with sparse Chebyshev polynomial graph filters, avoiding explicit eigendecomposition. When utilizing multilevel graph architectures, we introduce Graph Laplacian Energy Alignment for Meshes (GLEAM), which applies retained-subspace scale-aware supervision across graph hierarchies so that coarse and fine representations are regularized during autoregressive rollout. Our results show that the proposed spectral losses improve long-horizon rollout fidelity and preserve statistical invariants for the forecasting of turbulent flows on unstructured meshes, compared to deterministic baselines.

</details>


### [22] [Neural Operator Surrogates for Two-Dimensional Neutron Flux Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19388)
*Japan K. Patel,Barry D. Ganapol,Anthony Magliari,Matthew C. Schmidt,Todd A. Wareing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将一维单扫描神经算子研究扩展到二维，使用FNO和UNO两种神经算子构建三种代理模型来近似高保真中子输运问题中的标量通量，并评估单扫描输入和训练策略对精度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的研究仅限于一维单扫描神经算子，需要将该方法扩展到更实际的二维情况；同时需要评估不同神经算子架构和训练策略在解决中子输运屏蔽问题时对计算精度的影响，特别是在强衰减区域的精度问题。

Method: 采用两种神经算子架构：傅里叶神经算子(FNO)和U型神经算子(UNO)；构建三种代理模型：(1)FNO直接映射材料和源场到通量；(2)UNO直接映射；(3)FNO结合单扫描近似作为额外输入。使用验证过的离散纵坐标求解器生成高保真训练数据，采用相对L2误差范数评估精度，通过三个随机种子训练以评估结果稳定性。

Result: 通过对比三种代理模型的平均相对L2误差范数，评估了单扫描输入是否比直接映射提高精度，以及在对数域训练是否能改善强衰减区域的精度；分析了不同随机种子训练结果的运行间变异性。

Conclusion: 成功将单扫描神经算子方法从一维扩展到二维，为三维中子输运问题的快速高精度求解提供了可行方案；为屏蔽计算等强衰减区域的神经网络训练策略提供了指导。

Abstract: This work extends our one-dimensional single-sweep neural-operator studies to two dimensions. We consider one-group transport with isotropic scattering. As in the one-dimensional work, we use Fourier neural operators (FNOs) to approximate the high-fidelity scalar flux. Additionally, we also investigate U-shaped neural operators (UNOs) in this study. We consider three surrogates. The first two map the material and source fields directly to the flux, one using an FNO and one using a UNO. The third is an FNO that additionally takes the scalar flux after one source iteration, the single-sweep approximation, as an input. Each case is solved to high fidelity with a verified discrete-ordinates solver, and an average relative L_2 error norm is used to characterize the quality of the inferred maps. We train every surrogate over three random seeds so that differences between them can be assessed against run-to-run variability. Two questions guide the study: whether the single-sweep input improves accuracy over the direct maps, and whether training on the logarithm of the flux improves accuracy in the strongly attenuated regions relevant to shielding.

</details>


### [23] [The Orthogonalized Read Is a Removable Training Scaffold for Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19390)
*Keston Aquino-Michaels*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在mLSTM的噪声关联回忆任务中，训练时对记忆矩阵进行正交化读取能显著提升性能，但本文证明这不是架构改进，而是通过重新调节学习问题实现的效果。失败模型实际上已存储约一半关联，正交化读取帮助解决了读取失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现对mLSTM记忆矩阵在读取时进行正交化处理（通过Newton-Schulz迭代）能显著改善噪声关联回忆性能。作者旨在揭示这一现象背后的真实机制，判断其究竟是真正的架构改进还是其他学习动态机制。

Method: 通过三个关键属性验证假设：(1)自洽性：使用精确递归最小二乘读取（Mesa层）能复现效果，而直通半步、delta规则写入、冻结随机键和普通归一化都失败；(2)均匀性：在学习率×难度网格上，逃逸风险增加约6倍且无难度依赖；(3)可移除性：对失败模型推理时应用无效，在逃逸触发调度中退火后恢复为标准mLSTM且保持完整精度。直接解码记忆状态分析失败模型的存储内容。

Result: 正交化读取并非架构改进，而是通过在训练平台期重新调节学习问题来增加逃逸风险。失败模型实际上已存储约一半关联（线性可恢复形式），平台期是对半写入存储的读取失败。解决率遵循热噪声定律（学习率弹性+3.0，梯度噪声弹性-1.65），原始vocab-96结果是大批量噪声条件而非容量问题。

Conclusion: 两个超越具体干预的结论：(1)用于架构选择的回忆基准部分测量的是可训练性；(2)该系统是"涌现"的完整工具化模型生物体，其中急剧的行为阈值可证明源于对逐渐累积结构的截断度量。这揭示了架构评估中潜在的误导因素和深度学习系统中涌现现象的机制。

Abstract: A recent report finds that orthogonalizing the mLSTM memory matrix at read time (five Newton-Schulz iterations, trained through) substantially improves noisy associative recall. The effect replicates, but it is not a memory improvement. Training on this task is a long chance plateau followed by a sharp escape, and the orthogonalized read acts by re-conditioning the learning problem during the plateau. Three properties establish this. It must be self-consistent: an exact recursive least-squares read (the Mesa layer) reproduces it, while straight-through halves, delta-rule writes, frozen random keys, and plain normalization all fail. It is uniform: across a learning-rate x hardness grid it multiplies the escape hazard roughly six-fold with no detectable hardness dependence, widening the workable learning-rate corridor that narrows for the baseline. And it is removable: applied to failed models at inference it rescues none, and annealed away on an escape-triggered schedule it leaves numerically stock mLSTMs at full accuracy. Much of the published gain needs no architecture at all -- solved-rate at a fixed budget measures escape hazard, which follows a heat/noise law (learning-rate elasticity +3.0, gradient-noise elasticity -1.65) under which the original vocab-96 result is a large-batch noise condition rather than a capacity one. Decoding the memory state directly shows failed models carry roughly half their associations in linearly recoverable form: the plateau is a readout failure over half-written storage. Two conclusions travel beyond the intervention: recall benchmarks used for architecture selection partly measure trainability, and the system is a fully instrumented model organism of "emergence," in which a sharp behavioral threshold demonstrably arises from a censored metric over gradually accumulating structure.

</details>


### [24] [LAARA: Layer-Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19391)
*Ashutosh Tripathi,Surya Deep Singh,Pranab Sahoo,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAARA框架，通过Fisher信息动态分配不同transformer层的适配器秩(rank)，解决了现有方法对所有层统一分配秩的次优问题，在减少可训练参数的同时达到或超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低秩适应(LoRA)方法对所有transformer层分配相同的适配器秩，但不同层实际上具有不同的适应需求。本文从理论和实验角度证明统一秩分配从根本上是不优的，需要根据各层的实际适应需求动态分配秩。

Method: 提出LAARA (Layer Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation framework)框架：1) 在训练过程中使用轻量级对角Fisher估计量动态分配秩；2) 结合投影方向归一化、对数压缩、混合适配器重要性估计等技术；3) 引入vote-to-change阻尼机制确保稳定且高效的秩适应。该框架无需额外搜索过程。

Result: 在GLUE和MathInstruct基准测试上，LAARA在显著减少可训练参数的情况下，始终达到或超越LoRA、AdaLoRA、DyLoRA、Bitfit等主流方法的表现。实验证明了基于Fisher信息的秩分配方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Fisher引导的秩分配为自适应参数高效微调提供了有原则且有效的基础，LAARA框架通过层感知的动态秩分配实现了更优的性能-参数效率平衡。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation is widely used for parameter-efficient fine-tuning, yet existing methods typically assign the same adapter rank to every transformer layer despite their heterogeneous adaptation requirements. In this work, we show theoretically and empirically that uniform rank allocation is fundamentally suboptimal. Motivated by this observation, we propose LAARA (Layer Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation framework), a search-free framework that dynamically allocates ranks using lightweight diagonal Fisher estimates computed during training. LAARA combines projection-wise normalization, logarithmic compression, blended adapter importance estimation, and a vote-to-change dampening mechanism to produce stable and efficient rank adaptation. Experiments on GLUE and MathInstruct benchmark demonstrate that LAARA consistently matches or outperforms popular state of the art approaches such as LoRA, AdaLoRA, DyLoRA, and Bitfit while using significantly fewer trainable parameters. Our results show that Fisher-guided rank allocation provides a principled and effective foundation for adaptive parameter-efficient fine-tuning. The code is publicly available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LAARA-D305/LAARA.py

</details>


### [25] [Predicting Groundwater Arsenic Concentrations Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19392)
*William Xing,Stephanie Yang,Aarush Bandemegal,Anushree Misra,Ananya Kalapatapu,Brennan Lagasse,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了包含74,000多个砷样本的空间集成数据集，并证明图神经网络(GNN)通过考虑空间依赖性，能够匹配或超越梯度提升树在地下水砷浓度预测任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下水砷污染是美国长期存在的公共健康危机，特别是对依赖私人井的家庭。准确且具有空间信息的砷浓度预测对于识别高风险区域和集中缓解措施至关重要，但目前缺乏可泛化的模型来表示跨区域砷浓度的连续变化。

Method: 将砷预测建模为回归任务，整合Water Quality Portal (WQP)、Mineral Resources Data System (MRDS)和Gridded National Soil Survey Geographic Database (gNATSGO)的数据，使用k近邻(k-NN)和地理信息系统(GIS)技术按位置连接全美砷测量点，构建空间集成数据集。在此基础上评估多种机器学习模型，包括基于树的集成方法、多层感知机和空间感知图神经网络(GNN)。

Result: 虽然梯度提升树在表格数据领域仍被视为最先进方法，但图神经网络能够进一步考虑空间依赖性，从而匹配或超越梯度提升树的结果。

Conclusion: 基于图的空间感知学习能够增强环境预测能力，为改进地下水风险制图和监测提供基础。

Abstract: Arsenic contamination in groundwater presents a longstanding public health crisis in the United States, especially for households depending on private wells. Accurate and spatially informed prediction of arsenic concentration is vital to identify high-risk areas and focus mitigation efforts. However, there is a lack of generalizable models for representing continuous variation in arsenic concentrations across regions. In this work, we pose arsenic prediction as a regression task and construct a spatially integrated dataset to aggregate over 74,000 arsenic samples from the Water Quality Portal (WQP), Mineral Resources Data System (MRDS), and Gridded National Soil Survey Geographic Database (gNATSGO). Specifically, we use a variety of techniques including kNearest Neighbors (k-NN) and Geographic Information Systems (GIS) to join arsenic measurement points from across the United States by location. Building on this dataset, we evaluate a diverse suite of machine learning models, including tree-based ensemble approaches, multilayer perceptrons, and spatially aware graph neural networks (GNN). Our findings show that while gradient-boosted trees are still considered state-of-the-art in the field of tabular data, GNNs are able to further account for spatial dependence to match or outperform the results of gradient-boosted trees. These results demonstrate that graph-based and spatially informed learning can enhance environmental prediction and provide a foundation for improved groundwater risk mapping and monitoring.

</details>


### [26] [Decodable but Not Detectable: A Leakage Fingerprint for Near-OOD Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19393)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现文档基准测试中的OOD检测器存在基准泄漏问题（"OOD"类实际在训练集中），删除污染类后AUROC从0.326提升至0.911。提出了泄漏指纹诊断方法（监督解码率近1但无监督检测低于0.65），在52个设置中验证了有效性，并修正了评估协议，发现扰动信号可解码但不可检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在审计基于扰动的OOD检测器时发现AUROC仅为0.326（低于0.5的随机水平），这反常的性能促使作者调查根本原因，发现基准测试存在泄漏问题，需要开发诊断方法并建立正确的评估协议。

Method: 1) 识别基准泄漏：发现指定的"OOD"类实际在训练集中；2) 提出泄漏指纹：近完美监督解码性（AUROC≈1）+无监督检测崩溃（<0.65）；3) 在52个控制设置（20个泄漏、32个干净）上验证诊断方法；4) 使用ResNet-50和ViT-B/16在CIFAR-10/100上进行嵌入空间验证；5) 对24个标准近/远OOD基准对进行野外审计。

Result: 1) 删除污染类并重新训练35个模型后，AUROC从0.326提升至0.911；2) 泄漏诊断达到灵敏度18/20、特异度31/32，匹配的拟合集排除控制达到20/20完美；3) 野外审计仅在CIFAR-100 vs CIFAR-10对上触发，无远OOD对触发；4) 修正协议下，监督读者可恢复OOD信号（AUROC 0.87-1.00）但无监督检测器无法检测；5) 扰动方法未超越标准Mahalanobis距离。

Conclusion: 基准泄漏会导致OOD检测评估严重失真，本文提出的泄漏指纹诊断方法能有效识别此类问题。在修正的协议下，扰动信号虽可被监督方法解码但无法被无监督检测器识别，扰动方法未超越标准方法。贡献在于修正的评估协议和验证的泄漏诊断工具，而非新的OOD方法，同时撤回了早期的循环相关性结论。

Abstract: While auditing a perturbation-based OOD detector on a document benchmark, we recorded an AUROC of 0.326 -- well below the 0.5 chance level. The cause is a benchmark leak: the designated "OOD" class is one the model was trained on, so its examples sit inside the in-distribution fit set and the detector is penalized for correctly ranking them as familiar. Deleting the class and retraining 35 models across two domains raises the score to 0.911. We distill the contamination into a leak fingerprint -- near-perfect supervised decodability (AUROC approximately 1) coupled with unsupervised detection collapsed below 0.65 -- and validate it on a controlled battery of 52 settings (20 leaked, 32 clean) across ResNet-50 and ViT-B/16 on CIFAR-10/100, achieving sensitivity 18/20 and specificity 31/32 in embedding space; the matched fit-set-exclusion controls are perfect at 20/20. An in-the-wild audit of 24 standard near/far OOD benchmark pairs fires on exactly one (the intrinsically hard CIFAR-100 vs CIFAR-10 pair) and on no far-OOD pair, confirming specificity and that standard cross-dataset construction is clean. Under the corrected protocol, perturbation signals are decodable but not detectable: a supervised reader recovers the OOD signal (AUROC 0.87-1.00) while no unsupervised detector does, and the perturbation method does not improve on plain Mahalanobis distance. We provide a theoretical account of why and, for transparency, retract an earlier circular correlation. The contributions are a corrected protocol and a validated leak diagnostic, not a new OOD method.

</details>


### [27] [Cross-Subject Semantic Decoding with Shared-Space Alignment for Generalized Neural Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19394)
*Ji-Hoon Heo,Aleksandra Joanna Wisniewska,Seo-Hyun Lee,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种跨受试者语义解码框架，通过对齐多受试者的神经响应到共享潜在空间，实现了无需重新训练的跨受试者泛化，显著提升了语音感知的神经解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侵入性神经记录中的跨受试者泛化仍然具有挑战性，因为电极配置、解剖结构和神经信号模式在不同个体间存在显著差异，这种受试者间变异性阻碍了模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出跨受试者语义解码框架：1）使用共享响应模型将多受试者的皮层脑电图数据对齐到共享潜在空间；2）训练解码器从投影的神经表示预测上下文语义嵌入；3）对新的受试者，估计受试者特定的投影到预定义共享空间，直接应用预训练解码器而无需重新训练。

Result: 该框架在所有评估设置中均优于基线方法，并且在从源受试者到留出受试者测试时表现出更小的性能下降，表明跨受试者泛化能力得到改善。

Conclusion: 将神经活动对齐到共享潜在空间并在语义嵌入空间中进行解码，是一种有效的跨受试者泛化策略，能够在有效捕获共享的刺激相关表示的同时减少神经响应中的受试者特定差异。

Abstract: Generalizing across subjects remains challenging in invasive neural recordings because electrode configurations, anatomical structures, and neural signal patterns vary substantially across individuals. To investigate such inter-subject variability, we propose a cross-subject semantic decoding framework that aligns neural responses to speech perception from multiple subjects into a shared latent space and learns a mapping from the aligned neural representations to contextual embeddings. More specifically, using electrocorticography data collected during natural language comprehension, we estimate the shared space using the shared response model and train a decoder to predict contextual semantic embeddings from projected neural responses. For a held-out subject, we estimate a subject-specific projection into the predefined shared space, and directly apply the pretrained decoder without any retraining. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms baseline methods across evaluation settings and exhibits a reduced performance drop from source subject to held-out subject testing, indicating improved cross-subject generalization. These results suggest that aligning neural activity into a shared latent space, while decoding in a semantic embedding space, provides an effective strategy for improving cross-subject generalization by reducing subject-specific differences in neural responses while effectively capturing shared stimulus-related representations.

</details>


### [28] [From Trajectories to Prefixes: Reusing Teacher Trajectories via Replayed Prefixes and Online Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19395)
*Yihan Wang,Zhong Guan,Haoran Sun,Jiale Huang,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Prefix-GRPO强化学习框架，通过回放对齐的前缀查询和在线延续来训练小语言模型智能体，在多个任务上优于蒸馏和标准RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型作为交互式智能体骨干具有吸引力，但从强教师轨迹直接蒸馏会将丰富的多轮行为转化为单次模仿目标，这在早期决策塑造后续状态和奖励的长视野环境中效率低下。

Method: 提出Prefix-GRPO强化学习框架：(1)将教师轨迹分解为回放对齐的前缀查询和在线延续；(2)每个前缀在环境中回放以恢复有效的中间状态，学生继续在线交互并接收任务奖励；(3)对回放前缀内的历史助手token应用裁剪策略更新，使用策略蒸馏的SFT检查点估计其旧log概率，实现前缀学习和延续学习的统一优化。

Result: 在TextCraft、BabyAI和ALFWorld上的实验表明，Prefix-GRPO相比蒸馏和标准RL基线提升了小模型智能体性能；消融实验显示仅有回放而没有显式前缀token优化是不充分的。

Conclusion: Prefix-GRPO成功统一了前缀学习和延续学习，通过显式优化前缀token而非简单回放，有效提升了小语言模型在长视野多轮交互任务中的表现，为构建高效的小模型智能体提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Small language models are attractive backbones for interactive agents, but direct distillation from strong teacher trajectories often turns rich multi-turn behavior into one-shot imitation targets. This is inefficient in long-horizon environments, where early decisions shape later states and rewards. We propose Prefix-GRPO, a reinforcement learning framework that decomposes teacher trajectories into replay-aligned prefix queries and online continuations. Each prefix is replayed in the environment to recover a valid intermediate state, after which the student continues online interaction and receives task reward. Unlike response-only GRPO, Prefix-GRPO also applies clipped policy updates to historical assistant tokens inside the replayed prefix, using a policy-distilled SFT checkpoint to estimate their old log-probabilities. This unifies prefix learning and continuation learning within the same policy-optimization form. Experiments on TextCraft, BabyAI, and ALFWorld show that Prefix-GRPO improves small-model agents over distillation and standard RL baselines, while ablations show that replay alone is insufficient without explicit prefix-token optimization. The implementation and reproduction scripts are available at https://github.com/HappynessI/Prefix_GRPO.

</details>


### [29] [Memory Merge DQN: Sensitivity Weighted Target Updates for Stable Value Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19397)
*Adrian Ly,Richard Dazeley,Peter Vamplew,Sunil Aryal,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Memory Merge DQN方法，通过维护近期在线网络参数的历史记忆并基于Q值敏感性进行选择性参数融合来改进目标网络更新机制，在Atari游戏上超越了DQN、Averaged DQN和PQN等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DQN的目标网络采用硬拷贝更新策略，虽然短期稳定了引导值学习，但每次硬更新会突然丢弃近期参数历史，可能导致引导目标的突变并移除后续训练中有用的值函数结构。

Method: 提出Memory Merge DQN，维护近期在线网络副本的短时记忆，基于Q值敏感性而非仅拷贝最新在线网络来构建目标网络，赋予对当前Q值行为具有局部重要性的参数更大权重，并采用近期先验保持融合目标接近最新在线参数。

Result: 在Atari环境中与DQN、Averaged DQN、带层归一化的DQN及带梯度裁剪的PQN对比评估，Memory Merge DQN在最终性能上获得最多的第一名结果，击败所有对比基线，并在若干游戏中取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 选择性融合近期参数权重和历史能够提升DQN智能体的稳定性和最终性能，目标网络设计是长视界值学习过程中保留有用值函数结构的重要机制。

Abstract: Deep Q-networks use target networks to stabilise bootstrapped value learning, but the standard hard copy update also introduces a tradeoff. Holding the target network fixed, improves short term stability, yet each hard update abruptly replaces the target parameters with the newest online network and discards recent parameter history. This can produce sudden changes in the bootstrap target and may remove value function structure that remains useful later in training. This paper introduces Memory Merge DQN, a target network update mechanism that maintains a short memory of recent historical online network copies and constructs the target network by merging network parameters based on the Q-value sensitivity rather than copying only the newest online network. Memory Merge gives greater influence to parameters that remain locally important for current Q-value behaviour, while using a recency prior to keep the merged target close to the latest online parameters. The method is inspired by Fisher Weight Model Merging, but uses Q-value sensitivity rather than Fisher information as the weighting signal. This paper evaluates Memory Merge DQN on Atari environments against DQN, Averaged DQN, DQN with layer normalisation, and PQN (with gradient clipping). The results show that Memory Merge DQN is highly competitive and it achieves the largest number of first place final performance results among the evaluated methods, beats DQN, Averaged DQN, and PQN (with gradient clipping), and produces substantial gains in several games where preserving useful value-function parameters appears beneficial. These findings suggest that selectively merging recent parameter weights and history can improve the stability and final performance of DQN agents, and that target network design is an important mechanism for preserving useful value function structure during long horizon value learning.

</details>


### [30] [Leveraging Offline Supervision for Efficient and Generalizable Reinforcement Learning in Large-Scale Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19399)
*Dmitriy Poyarkov,Aleksei Staroverov,Aleksandr I. Panov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种离线-在线混合训练方法，通过在强化学习中引入离线监督（离线数据或预训练参考策略），在保持强OOD性能的同时将训练效率提升约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明在线强化学习比离线方法具有更好的OOD性能，但训练成本较高。研究者希望探索离线-在线混合训练能否结合两者优势，在保持强OOD能力的同时提高训练效率。

Method: 研究通过离线监督（离线数据或离线训练的参考策略）正则化的强化学习方法，在OOD基准测试上进行评估，并与纯离线训练和标准RL方法进行对比。

Result: 混合方法在保持与标准RL相近性能的同时，训练预算减少约一半；离线监督的引入保留了强OOD能力，未出现速度与OOD性能的权衡折衷。

Conclusion: 离线监督与在线RL的结合是有效的训练策略，能够在不牺牲OOD性能的前提下显著提升训练效率，为VLA策略训练提供了更优的方案。

Abstract: It is commonly observed that online reinforcement learning (RL) produces better-performing strategies than offline methods across a broad range of performance measures. In particular, RL-trained policies exhibit stronger out-of-distribution (OOD) behavior, where models trained only with imitation learning approaches often struggle. A recent study introduced an OOD-focused benchmark and reported that RL-trained vision-language-action (VLA) policies achieve noticeably better OOD performance and slightly better in-distribution (IND) performance than their counterparts trained with supervised fine-tuning (SFT). In this work, we investigate whether hybrid offline-online training can combine the advantages of both approaches. Specifically, we study RL methods regularized by offline supervision via either offline data or an offline-trained reference policy. We evaluate these approaches on the OOD benchmark and compare them with both offline-only training and standard RL. Our results show that although offline training achieves limited OOD performance by itself, incorporating offline supervision into RL preserves strong OOD capability while substantially improving training efficiency. In particular, the guided methods reach performance close to that of standard RL while requiring roughly half of the training budget. Rather than producing a trade-off between speed and OOD performance, the hybrid approach retains strong OOD capability while achieving this efficiency gain. Project page: https://alstar8.github.io/offline-supervision-vla-rl

</details>


### [31] [Predictive single cell foundation model for gene regulation and aging with privacy-preserving tabular learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19400)
*Jiayuan Ding,Jianhui Lin,Ziyang Miao,Nils Mechtel,Shiyu Jiang,Yixin Wang,Zhaoyu Fang,Jorge D. Martin-Rufino,Chen Weng,Reuben Saunders,Weize Xu,Jonathan S. Weissman,Min Li,Jiliang Tang,Wei Ouyang,Yuancheng Ryan Lu,Xiaojie Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Tabula，这是一个隐私保护的单细胞基础模型，它采用联邦学习并明确建模单细胞数据的表格结构，同时开发了去中心化AI代理平台Chiron用于协作训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单细胞基础模型在扩展时引发隐私担忧，并且忽略了单细胞数据独特的无序表格结构，这些结构特征不同于文本数据。需要开发既能保护隐私又能正确处理单细胞数据结构特征的新型基础模型。

Method: 提出了Tabula，一个采用联邦学习技术的隐私保护基础模型，显式建模单细胞数据的表格结构；同时开发了Chiron去中心化AI代理平台，支持跨机构协作训练而无需共享原始数据。采用年龄和身份评分引导的计算机优先化方法进行 rejuvenation 因子筛选。

Result: 在下游基准测试中表现优异；在造血、胰腺发生、神经发生和心脏发生等多种生物系统中揭示组合调控逻辑；使用年轻和年老成纤维细胞配对scRNA-seq数据集，成功筛选出 rejuvenation 因子，性能超越传统方法。

Conclusion: Tabula通过整合表格学习与联邦学习，代表了单细胞基础建模的重要进展，为人类健康领域的隐私保护虚拟细胞铺平了道路。

Abstract: Pre-trained foundation models (FMs) have begun transforming single-cell genomics, but scaling them raises privacy concerns. Moreover, unlike text data, single-cell data is unordered and exhibits a unique tabular structure that current single-cell FMs overlook. We introduce Tabula, a privacy-preserving FM designed with federated learning (FL) that explicitly models the tabular structure of single-cell data. To deploy Tabula, we further developed Chiron, a decentralized AI agent-enabled platform for collaborative training across institutions without sharing raw data. Beyond strong performance across downstream benchmarks, Tabula reveals combinatorial regulatory logic across diverse biological systems, including hematopoiesis, pancreatic endogenesis, neurogenesis, and cardiogenesis. Using a new scRNA-seq dataset of paired young and aged human fibroblasts, Tabula nominates rejuvenation factors through age- and identity score-guided in silico prioritization, outperforming conventional approaches. Thus, Tabula represents an important advance in single-cell foundation modeling by integrating tabular learning with FL, paving the way toward privacy-preserving virtual cells for human health.

</details>

*Renjie He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对共识分割方法进行了系统性理论分析，发现STAPLE算法在常见条件下退化为阈值多数投票，存在95%的EM次优性且在类别不平衡下失效。研究建议重新审视简单稳健的多数投票基线方法，并证明结合图像信息的深度共识模型和共形预测可提供更可靠的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割领域普遍默认使用STAPLE等"复杂"共识方法，但缺乏对其理论基础和局限性的系统分析。研究者希望从第一性原理出发建立共识分割的数学基础，通过受控实验验证理论预测，揭示现有方法的实际问题并推动更原则性的方法发展。

Method: 从第一性原理推导共识分割的数学基础，包括生成模型、EM算法、Van Leemput边际化分析、可识别性条件、Spatial STAPLE和深度变分公式。通过受控实验系统验证每个理论预测，分析STAPLE在典型医学图像场景下的表现，并与多数投票基线进行比较。同时探索深度共识模型结合图像信息的方法，以及共形预测提供不确定性保证的可行性。

Result: 核心发现：在常见条件下，STAPLE退化为阈值多数投票，存在95%的EM次优性，在类别不平衡时崩溃。这些问题不是边缘情况而是医学图像中的典型场景。多数投票作为简单、非参数、稳健的基线方法表现意外强劲。深度共识模型证明当结合图像与标签时共识问题变得可处理，共形预测可实现实用的形式化不确定性保证。

Conclusion: 研究建议从业者批判性评估共识方法而非默认使用STAPLE。多数投票不应被轻视，深度共识模型和共形预测为更原则性的方法提供了理论和实证基础。研究为共识分割领域提供了完整的数学和实证分析框架。

Abstract: This paper provides a rigorous, self-contained investigation of consensus segmentation. We derive the mathematical foundations from first principles -- the generative model, EM algorithm, Van Leemput's marginalization analysis, identifiability conditions, Spatial STAPLE, and deep variational formulations -- and validate each theoretical prediction through controlled experiments. The central finding is sobering: under common conditions, STAPLE reduces to thresholded majority voting, suffers 95% EM suboptimality, and collapses under class imbalance. These are not edge cases but typical scenarios in medical imaging. Majority voting -- simple, non-parametric, and robust -- is a surprisingly strong baseline that the field has perhaps too hastily dismissed in favor of more "sophisticated" methods. At the same time, the deep consensus model demonstrates that the consensus problem is not inherently difficult -- it becomes tractable when the image is used alongside the labels. And conformal prediction shows that formal uncertainty guarantees are achievable and practical. We hope this work encourages practitioners to critically evaluate their consensus methods rather than applying STAPLE by default, and provides the mathematical and empirical foundation for more principled approaches.

</details>


### [33] [Recovering Clinical Utility Under Differential Privacy: Empirical Validation of Adaptive Federated Aggregation on Heterogeneous Cardiovascular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19403)
*Rodrigo Tertulino,Laercio Alencar,Ricardo Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在真实心血管疾病数据集上验证了FedCVR联邦学习框架，证明了服务器端自适应优化在差分隐私设置下的有效性，F1-score达到79.2%，AUC达到0.96，显著优于标准FedAvg方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究仅在合成数据上证明了FedCVR框架中服务器端自适应优化能够作为差分隐私噪声的时间去噪器，缺乏在真实临床数据上的验证。为填补从概念验证到实际医疗部署之间的空白，本研究旨在真实多中心心血管数据集上验证该框架的可行性和有效性。

Method: 研究使用5个公开的真实心血管疾病数据集（Framingham、Cleveland、Hungarian、Switzerland和Long Beach VA），统一为13属性的UCI心脏病模式，构建异构联邦学习场景。采用留一机构交叉验证方法，在噪声乘数为0.8、隐私预算epsilon约4.2的操作条件下，对比评估FedCVR与标准FedAvg方法的性能。

Result: FedCVR在真实数据上保持了自适应优势，F1-Score达到79.2%，AUC达到0.96。在所有评估指标上统计显著优于标准FedAvg（配对t检验，所有p <= 0.003，在Bonferroni校正阈值下显著）。真实数据上的隐私损失测量结果验证了合成实验中观察到的渐进退化模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果为FedCVR框架在真实多中心医疗环境中的临床可行性提供了实证证据，成功弥合了合成环境概念验证与实际医疗部署之间的差距，为联邦学习在医疗领域的应用奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Validating federated learning frameworks on real clinical data is an essential step between proof-of-concept demonstrations in controlled synthetic environments and deployment in real multicenter healthcare settings. A prior architectural study by the same authors (Tertulino and Alencar, 2026) demonstrated, on a synthetic six-feature benchmark, that server-side adaptive optimization acts as a temporal denoiser for Differential Privacy noise, answering an open challenge identified in the original pipeline work (Tertulino, 2025). That study used synthetically generated data and explicitly identified real-world validation as a priority future direction. The present work addresses this gap by validating the FedCVR framework on five publicly available real cardiovascular datasets (Framingham, Cleveland, Hungarian, Switzerland, and Long Beach VA), harmonized to the 13-attribute UCI Heart Disease schema and configured as a heterogeneous federated scenario with leave-one-institution-out cross-validation. Results demonstrate that FedCVR preserves its adaptive advantage on real data, achieving an F1-Score of 79.2% and AUC of 0.96 under the operational privacy budget (noise multiplier = 0.8, privacy budget epsilon approximately 4.2), while statistically outperforming standard FedAvg on all evaluated metrics (paired t-tests, all p <= 0.003, significant under the Bonferroni-corrected threshold). The measured privacy cost on real data confirms the graceful degradation pattern observed in the synthetic experiments, providing empirical evidence of the framework's clinical viability in genuine multicenter contexts.

</details>


### [34] [Structured Latent Space Modeling over Multi-Scale Temporal Patches for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19404)
*Xingsheng Chen,Deyu Yi,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出M2Patch，一种基于CNN的多变量时间序列预测架构，通过多尺度补丁和可分离卷积将观测映射到结构化潜在空间，利用尺度内平滑性和尺度间对齐约束组织潜在表示，在10个基准测试中取得了显著优于基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测骨干网络将学习到的表示视为预测的临时副产品，忽略了对多变量时间序列中跨多时间尺度展开的结构模式的组织几何特性的利用。

Method: M2Patch采用多尺度补丁将输入分解为重叠的时间粒度；使用具有渐进式膨胀的深度可分离卷积以线性时间提取尺度特定特征；通过尺度特定的学习投影将特征压缩为紧凑的潜在表示。潜在空间通过尺度内平滑性约束（强制相邻补丁间的时间连续性）和尺度间对齐约束（通过可学习的跨尺度映射实现）进行组织。

Result: 在10个真实世界基准测试的40个预测设置中，M2Patch取得了57个最佳结果和34个次佳结果，在大多数基准测试上达到或超过代表性基线，同时保持线性计算复杂度和对补丁级输入损坏的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: M2Patch成功地将多变量时间序列映射到结构化潜在空间，有效利用了跨多时间尺度的结构模式，实现了预测性能的显著提升，同时保持了计算效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multivariate time series encode structural patterns that unfold across multiple temporal scales, yet most forecasting backbones treat learned representations as transient byproducts of prediction, leaving the organizational geometry of these patterns underexploited. We introduce M2Patch, a CNN-based forecasting architecture that maps channel-independent multivariate observations into a structured latent space through two complementary differentiable constraints. Multi-scale patching decomposes the input into overlapping temporal granularities; depthwise separable convolutions with progressive dilation extract scale-specific features in linear time; and per-scale learned projections compress these features into a compact latent representation. The latent space is organized by an intra-scale smoothness constraint that enforces temporal continuity between adjacent patches, and an inter-scale alignment constraint, realized through learnable cross-scale mappings, that restores cross-granularity interaction within the channel-independent design, ensuring that all scales encode mutually consistent representations of the underlying dynamics. Experiments on ten real-world benchmarks show that M2Patch achieves 57 best and 34 second-best results across 40 forecasting settings, matching or exceeding representative baselines on most benchmarks while maintaining linear computational complexity and robustness to patch-level input corruption.

</details>
